SELECT CUTDET.DESIGNNO,MAX(SCREEN) AS [SCREEN_],MAX(sCREEN) AS ALTQUAL, MAX(CCUT) AS [CUT], ROUND(SUM(WMTS*RATE+RMTS*JOBRATE)/SUM(CPCS-SECONDS),2) AS [RATE_], CUTDET.DESIGNNO AS CUTCARDNO,MAX(MILL) AS PACK,CUTDET.DESIGNNO AS [REF NO],MAX(CATEGORY) AS [CATEGORY_], SUM(CPCS-SECONDS)-(CASE WHEN SUMPCS2 IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE SUMPCS2 END) AS [PEND PCS],SUM(CPCS-SECONDS) AS [TOT PCS],0 AS [MISC1],(SUM(CPCS-SECONDS)-(CASE WHEN SUMPCS2 IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE SUMPCS2 END))*MAX(CCUT) AS [PEND MTS],MAX(MILL) AS [PARTY NAME],'' AS RMK,0 AS CNO,'PCS' AS UNIT,max(greyqual) as [GREY QUALITY] FROM CUTDET LEFT JOIN (SELECT DESIGNNO,SUM(PCS) AS SUMPCS2 FROM SAREEDES WHERE SAREEDES.TYPE LIKE 'S%' OR SAREEDES.TYPE LIKE 'O%' GROUP BY DESIGNNO) AS DESIGNTOT ON CUTDET.DESIGNNO=DESIGNTOT.DESIGNNO GROUP BY CUTDET.DESIGNNO,SUMPCS2 HAVING (SUM(CPCS-SECONDS)>SUMPCS2 OR SUMPCS2 IS NULL) AND SUM(CPCS-SECONDS)>0 ORDER BY CUTDET.DESIGNNOSELECT PINVTRN.VNO,PINVTRN.DATE,PINVTRN.CODE,pinvtrn.bill, PINVTRN.QUAL AS QUALITY, PINVTRN.AVG_WT, PINVTRN.RATE , totPCS,RPCS,OTHERPCS, TOTMTS,RMTS,OTHERMTS, PINVTRN.BCODE, PINVTRN.CHECKER, PINVTRN.CODE, PINVTRN.ORDERNO,PINVTRN.SENTMTS FROM (bills AS PINVTRN LEFT JOIN (select cno,[type],vno,sum(wpcs) as rpcs,sum(wmts) as rmts,max(ddate) as recdate from pinvtrn WHERE MILL<>'GODOWN STOCK' group by cno,[type],vnO) AS recdet ON (pinvtrn.cno=recdet.cno) AND (pinvtrn.type=recdet.type) AND (pinvtrn.vno=recdet.vno)) LEFT JOIN (SELECT CNO,[TYPE],PVNO,COUNT(*) AS OTHERPCS,SUM(CHALTRN.PMTS) AS OTHERMTS FROM CHALTRN INNER JOIN PINVTRN ON CHALTRN.CARDNO=PINVTRN.CARDNO WHERE (PINVTRN.VNO=0 OR PINVTRN.VNO IS NULL) AND CHALTRN.PVNO<>0 AND PINVTRN.TYPE='P1' GROUP BY PINVTRN.CNO,PINVTRN.TYPE,CHALTRN.PVNO) AS CHALSENT ON (PINVTRN.VNO=CHALSENT.PVNO) AND (PINVTRN.TYPE=CHALSENT.TYPE) AND (PINVTRN.CNO=CHALSENT.CNO) WHERE PINVTRN.CNO = 4 And PINVTRN.TYPE = 'P1' And PINVTRN.TOTMTS > 0 ORDER BY PINVTRN.CNO, PINVTRN.TYPE, PINVTRN.DATE, PINVTRN.VNO
